# CHAOS REDUX - FALLOUT REVIEWED CANDIDATE PILOT EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# This file owns the first reviewed ordinary-candidate producer for the Fallout
# scheduler. It installs the manually authored global-survival rows.
# Food has a country target. Water, rail, Market Under the Viaduct, Well Queue,
# and Animal Feed carry one
# selected state target only when their native source proof is present. The
# producer never sets a scheduler activation flag and never fires an event by
# itself.
# ============================================================================

# has_resources_amount takes a file-scoped integer for its amount operand.
@FALLOUT_MINE_RESOURCE_PRESENCE_AMOUNT = 0

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Initializes one candidate row's common typed
# payload. The caller then overwrites only the pilot-specific fields.
fallout_event_initialize_pilot_candidate_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_class = constant:fallout_event_class.routine_incident }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_primary_family = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.global_survival_and_society }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.food_security }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_human_token = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_ai_token = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_branch = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase_mode = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.neutral }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_season }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_secondary_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_mode = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_authored_adjustment = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_pressure_source_type = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.survival_resource }

	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity_source = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure_source = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_player_relevance_source = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }

	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_resource_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_character_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }

	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_air_winter_phase = constant:fallout_event_air_winter_phase.phase_zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.none }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	# Relationship rows must replace this sentinel only after a reviewed
	# reciprocal payload and back-reference have been authored.
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_partner_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_parent_arc_ticket = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_ticket }

	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_country_memory = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_cause_memory = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_winter = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_resource = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_character = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_route = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_player_relevance = constant:fallout_event_runtime.player_relevance_human_recipient }
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY. Appends the current typed temporary payload to all
# parallel candidate arrays. A row is never appended through a partial branch.
fallout_event_append_pilot_candidate_row = {
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_transaction_key_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_class_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_class }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_primary_family_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_primary_family }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_cooldown_family_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_human_event_token_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_human_token }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_ai_event_token_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_ai_token }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_branch_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_branch }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_phase_mode_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_phase_mode }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_phase_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_phase }
	add_to_array = { array = fallout_event_candidate_secondary_phase_entries value = fallout_event_pilot_secondary_phase }

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. Builds one generation-bound candidate
# registry row set per Fallout country. State targets are selected by the
# lowest valid native state id, and a missing native source omits that row.
fallout_event_build_pilot_candidate_registries = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = fallout_event_registry_member
				fallout_event_registry_country_row_is_current = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_event_candidate_registry_reviewed }
					NOT = { has_variable = fallout_event_candidate_registry_generation }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_candidate_registry_generation value = fallout_event_runtime_generation compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			fallout_event_clear_candidate_rows_for_rebuild = yes

			# The country-level food row is always available after the numerical
			# registry is current. Its chain carries no invented state target.
			fallout_event_initialize_pilot_candidate_row = yes
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.food_candidate_id }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.food_transaction_key }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.food_security }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_human_token = constant:fallout_event_id.last_inventory_human }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_ai_token = constant:fallout_event_id.last_inventory_ai }
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_resource_index = constant:fallout_survival_resource.food }

								AND = {
									check_variable = { var = fallout_event_366_state_score value = ROOT.fallout_event_366_candidate_reactor_score compare = equals }

			# The Door List row freezes one destination and one different source
			# receipt. Pair order is destination id, then source id. The source
			# receipt is state-owned so the shared candidate arrays do not need a
			# new parallel column.
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
			every_owned_state = {
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id = THIS.id }
				if = {
					limit = {
						fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination_is_current = yes
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
							check_variable = { var = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id value = fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination compare = less_than }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
					check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 value = constant:fallout_event_230_threshold.minimum_country_shelter compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^1 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.family_food compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.family_filters compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^3 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.specialist_medicine compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^8 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.specialist_shelter compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^9 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.lottery_recognition compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^7 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.lottery_filters compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						check_variable = { fallout_survival_current_entries^4 value = constant:fallout_event_230_cost.refusal_scrap compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_state_id = fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				var:fallout_event_pilot_door_list_destination = {
					save_event_target_as = fallout_event_230_candidate_destination
					set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_exposure = fallout_pretransition_air_winter_exposure }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_exposure_floor = fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_exposure }
				add_to_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_exposure_floor = constant:fallout_event_230_threshold.minimum_source_exposure_gap }
				every_owned_state = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id = THIS.id }
					if = {
						limit = {
							fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source_is_current = yes
							NOT = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id value = ROOT.fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_state_id compare = equals } }
							check_variable = { fallout_pretransition_air_winter_exposure value = ROOT.fallout_event_230_candidate_source_exposure_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							OR = {
								check_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
								check_variable = { var = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id value = ROOT.fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source compare = less_than }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source > constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_state_id = fallout_event_pilot_door_list_source }
					set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
					event_target:fallout_event_230_candidate_destination = {
						set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_state_id = ROOT.fallout_event_230_candidate_source_state_id }
						set_variable = { fallout_event_230_candidate_source_generation = ROOT.fallout_event_230_candidate_source_generation }
					}
					fallout_event_initialize_pilot_candidate_row = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.door_list_candidate_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.door_list_transaction_key }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_class = constant:fallout_event_class.crisis_incident }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.shelter }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_human_token = constant:fallout_event_id.door_list_human }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_ai_token = constant:fallout_event_id.door_list_ai }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_season }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_secondary_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.first_winter_year }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase_mode = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.preferred }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_230_transaction.visible_budget_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_resource_index = constant:fallout_survival_resource.shelter_capacity }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity = fallout_pretransition_air_winter_exposure }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity_source = fallout_event_230_candidate_source_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value = fallout_pretransition_air_winter_shelter_capacity }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value_source = fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target = fallout_event_230_candidate_destination_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.door_list_route }
					fallout_event_append_pilot_candidate_row = yes
				}
			}

					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure = fallout_failed_calculation_research_confidence }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^6 }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.survival_resource }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_air_winter_phase = air_winter_phase }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_government_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_winter_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_resource_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_archetype = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_winter = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_resource_index = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_resource = constant:fallout_event_831_threshold.minimum_country_power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_route = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target = fallout_event_831_candidate_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.failed_calculation_route }
					fallout_event_append_pilot_candidate_row = yes
				}
				clear_temp_variable = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id
				clear_variable = fallout_event_831_candidate_state_id
			}
			clear_variable = fallout_event_831_candidate_state_id

			# Laboratory Citizenship follows a closed Failed Calculation district.
			# It selects the lowest native host so universal rights, service tiers,
			# apprenticeship, and strict technocracy resolve against one civic state.
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = fallout_government_archetype
					check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype value = constant:fallout_government_archetype.technate compare = equals }
					has_variable = fallout_country_memory_id
					check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id value = constant:fallout_country_memory.manchurian_reactor_keeps compare = equals }
					check_variable = { fallout_region_id value = constant:fallout_region.east_asia compare = equals }
					has_country_flag = fallout_event_366_memory_closed
				}
				fallout_event_838_initialize_ledgers = yes
				set_variable = { fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				every_owned_state = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id = THIS.id }
					if = {
						limit = {
							fallout_event_pilot_laboratory_citizenship_state_is_current = yes
							OR = {
								check_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
								check_variable = { var = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id value = ROOT.fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id compare = less_than }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_838_country_is_current = yes }
					fallout_event_initialize_pilot_candidate_row = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.laboratory_citizenship_candidate_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.laboratory_citizenship_transaction_key }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_primary_family = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.government_archetype }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.archetype }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_human_token = constant:fallout_event_id.laboratory_citizenship_human }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_ai_token = constant:fallout_event_id.laboratory_citizenship_ai }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase_mode = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.preferred }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.rival_orders }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_secondary_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.generation_change }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_mode = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.eligible }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_838_transaction.visible_budget_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity = fallout_laboratory_citizenship_class_pressure_current }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure = fallout_laboratory_citizenship_civic_confidence }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^6 }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.survival_resource }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_air_winter_phase = air_winter_phase }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_government_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_winter_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_resource_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_archetype = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_winter = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_resource_index = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_resource = constant:fallout_event_838_threshold.minimum_country_power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_route = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target = fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.laboratory_citizenship_route }
					fallout_event_append_pilot_candidate_row = yes
				}
				clear_temp_variable = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id
				clear_variable = fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id
			}
			clear_variable = fallout_event_838_candidate_state_id

			# The Machine Suggests follows a closed Laboratory Citizenship district.
			# It selects the lowest native host so recommendation, sandbox, review,
			# and advisory choices resolve against one authenticated protocol state.
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = fallout_government_archetype
					check_variable = { fallout_government_archetype value = constant:fallout_government_archetype.technate compare = equals }
					has_variable = fallout_country_memory_id
					check_variable = { fallout_country_memory_id value = constant:fallout_country_memory.manchurian_reactor_keeps compare = equals }
					check_variable = { fallout_region_id value = constant:fallout_region.east_asia compare = equals }
					has_country_flag = fallout_event_366_memory_closed
				}
				fallout_event_845_initialize_ledgers = yes
				set_variable = { fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
				every_owned_state = {
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id = THIS.id }
					if = {
						limit = {
							fallout_event_pilot_machine_suggests_state_is_current = yes
							OR = {
								check_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
								check_variable = { var = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id value = ROOT.fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id compare = less_than }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { ROOT.fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { fallout_event_845_country_is_current = yes }
					fallout_event_initialize_pilot_candidate_row = yes
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_candidate_id = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.machine_suggests_candidate_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_transaction_key = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.machine_suggests_transaction_key }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_class = constant:fallout_event_class.major_arc }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_primary_family = constant:fallout_event_primary_family.government_archetype }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_cooldown_family = constant:fallout_event_cooldown_family.archetype }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_human_token = constant:fallout_event_id.machine_suggests_human }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_ai_token = constant:fallout_event_id.machine_suggests_ai }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase_mode = constant:fallout_event_review_phase_mode.preferred }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.rival_orders }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_secondary_phase = constant:fallout_event_phase.generation_change }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_mode = constant:fallout_event_crisis_break_mode.eligible }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_visible_budget_cost = constant:fallout_event_845_transaction.visible_budget_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity = fallout_machine_suggests_machine_risk_current }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_severity_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure = fallout_machine_suggests_machine_civic_confidence }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_mechanic_pressure_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.event_owned }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value = fallout_survival_current_entries^6 }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_state_value_source = constant:fallout_event_pressure_source_type.survival_resource }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_air_winter_phase = air_winter_phase }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_government_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_winter_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_crisis_resource_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route_match = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_archetype = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_winter = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_resource_index = constant:fallout_survival_resource.power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_resource = constant:fallout_event_845_threshold.minimum_country_power }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_required_route = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target_type = constant:fallout_event_subject_type.state }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_target = fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id }
					set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_pilot_route = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.machine_suggests_route }
					fallout_event_append_pilot_candidate_row = yes
				}
				clear_temp_variable = fallout_event_pilot_state_candidate_id
				clear_variable = fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id
			}
			clear_variable = fallout_event_845_candidate_state_id

			set_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_registry_generation = fallout_event_runtime_generation }
			set_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_registry_day = global.num_days }
			set_country_flag = fallout_event_candidate_registry_reviewed
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { fallout_event_candidate_registry_is_reviewed = yes } }
				clr_country_flag = fallout_event_candidate_registry_reviewed
				clear_variable = fallout_event_candidate_registry_generation
				clear_variable = fallout_event_candidate_registry_day
			}
		}
	}
}

# Supported scope: COUNTRY coordinator. This dormant pass pairs the two lowest
# current Refugee Train rows through the existing registry index ledger. It
# writes no tags, no diplomacy relation, and no scheduler activation flag.
# Every side must already have a reviewed candidate row before either partner
# index is written, so a missing or stale country leaves both rows unpaired.
fallout_event_pair_refugee_train_candidate_rows = {
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.fallout_event_registry_countries
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_event_candidate_registry_is_reviewed = yes
				any_of = {
					array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries
					value = fallout_event_refugee_pair_candidate_id
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_refugee_pair_candidate_id value = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.refugee_train_candidate_id compare = equals }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index } }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index compare = less_than }
				}
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index }
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
						check_variable = { var = fallout_event_registry_index value = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index = fallout_event_registry_index }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
			check_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index > constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
		}
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_index = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_index = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index }
	set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_mutation_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero }
	var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_index = {
		for_each_loop = {
			array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries
			value = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_id
			index = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_row_index
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_id value = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.refugee_train_candidate_id compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_refugee_candidate_row_index = fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_mutation_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
	var:global.fallout_event_registry_countries^fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_index = {
		for_each_loop = {
			array = fallout_event_candidate_id_entries
			value = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_id
			index = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_row_index
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = fallout_event_refugee_candidate_id value = constant:fallout_event_candidate_pilot.refugee_train_candidate_id compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { fallout_event_candidate_partner_registry_index_entries^fallout_event_refugee_candidate_row_index = fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_index }
				set_temp_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_mutation_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { fallout_event_refugee_pair_mutation_found = constant:fallout_event_runtime.zero } }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
		set_variable = { global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index = constant:fallout_event_runtime.no_registry_index }
	}
	}
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_first_registry_index
	clear_variable = global.fallout_event_refugee_pair_second_registry_index
}
